⚠️ **Two things DID fire today — do not read this file as a quiet day:** MIDAS-06 resolved to a **fired branch (a)** and palladium's **I2 re-open condition met (n=3)**, both §8. **Prome owns this file** (commits PROME-standard, explicit-path). Update after regime-level changes or >48h stale in a market week. **Re-base rule:** chain >~5 amendments or ~2 weeks → archive verbatim (crc in the snapshot header), rewrite current-state-only, re-verify carried claims. **Every re-base closes with a blind cold-reader verification before commit** (Opus; WQ-165 stop: ❌ = 0 OR two consecutive reads with only basis-and-pointer-class ⚠️). **Structure since 2026-09-02 (Will-ruled, `PROME/proposals/2026-09-02_heartbeat-split-RULED.md`): HOT = this file, one current-state paragraph per channel, ≤600 B each (a TARGET the gate does not yet meter — re-cut at every re-base), THE level table, thresholds, pointers; COLD = `PROME/HEARTBEAT_COLD.md`, long-forms by channel. A correction that needs prose goes to COLD; the hot cell gets the corrected figure and a `→ cold §N`.** P1 read-cap: boot-read-whole surface under 32,550 B (rotate ≥75% / stop <70%; `prome_gate` meters it) — history goes to the snapshots at every re-base, never accretes here. Rule provenance → `docs/CANON_PROVENANCE.md` + `PROME/proposals/2026-08-23_heartbeat-gate-RULED.md`.

## §1.3 — Energy, 2026-09-10 night → 09-11 adds (long-form for the fourteenth-base hot §1; sources = the owner artifacts named)

> ⛔ **CORRECTION — 2026-09-12 (PROME, consuming BRENT `635631fde` + FALCON's 9/11 retraction). THE BODY BELOW IS PRESERVED AS THE 9/11 RECORD AND IS NOT REWRITTEN. Two of its load-bearing TOKENS are dead, and one of its figures now carries a caveat. The BG-02 and FAL-05 verdicts themselves are UNCHANGED and still correct — it is their BASIS that moved.**
>
> ⛔ **DEAD — *"VERIFIED ABSENCE at two primaries"* / *"no Aramco/MoE/SPA/CENTCOM statement = VERIFIED published negative"*.** The **Saudi MoE stated on Fri 2026-09-11 that the East-West/Petroline line is SHUT** ("precautionary"). BRENT has **retracted** the absence leg from its own 9/11 record. Anywhere below that reads an absence of a statement as the basis of a grade, the basis is gone. ⚠️ Force majeure remains **SEARCH-NOT-FOUND — which is NOT verified absence**, and that distinction is the whole lesson here.
> ⛔ **DEAD — *"FALCON tell #2 NOT FIRED, PENDING-CONFIRMATION"*.** Tell #2 **FIRED 2026-09-11** on FALCON's pre-committed resolver #1 (the MoE statement). ⚠️ No published time of day for the statement is on file at any artifact — do not quote one. ⚠️ **Multiple relays of ONE official statement are ONE source.**
> ⚠️ **CAVEATED — the *"~3.7 mb/d"* Yanbu baseline must never travel bare again. Carry it as `~3.7 mb/d [Vortexa; Kpler 2.9 same period]`.** BRENT found the vendor spread on its own baseline is **0.8 mb/d (Vortexa 3.7 vs Kpler 2.9, Sept MTD)** — **wider than the ≥0.7 mb/d R2 floor keyed to it**, so that resolver could be satisfied or refuted by choice of vendor alone. Worse than noise: **AIS trackers under-count one-directionally** (Vortexa recorded ~⅓ of one week's Yanbu volumes loading transponders-off), so a tracker prints a FALL whenever AIS-dark share RISES with **zero change in barrels** — and dark share rises exactly when the theater worsens, i.e. on the event class BG-02 gates. **The error is correlated with the trigger and biased toward FIRING.** → **WQ-234** (Will's call on whether an AIS-derived instrument belongs on a capital gate at all); BRENT has pre-registered a strictly restrictive dual-tracker repair so nothing sits unguarded meanwhile.
> ⚠️ **STILL UNESTABLISHED and not asserted anywhere: ATTRIBUTION · DAMAGE LOCATION · BARRELS.** Net supply loss is **UNQUANTIFIED**; nameplate ≠ routed flow ≠ qualifying offline capacity ≠ net supply loss. The route-geometry test is **INCONCLUSIVE** — FALCON's own population control refuted its 4.3 km agreement (median cross-track −57.7 km) — **no station is named.** BG-02's ≥0.7 mb/d 7-day-MA resolver still runs to **2026-09-25 17:00 ET** (DOCKET L329, a dated history row that is NOT refreshed).
- **The reported Petroline strike (~17:56Z 9/10).** What circulated: RT/EGYOSINT "six FIRMS hotspots"; investinglive "FRP >70 MW over ~8 hours across a stretch of only a few kilometres" (BRENT places the cluster SE of Medina); Daily Caller/Gulf News a Sentinel-3 plume "close to 100 kilometres long" posted by an Iranian account. **A few km and 100 km are not the same event, and Sentinel-3 is not FIRMS** — one claim, two instrument families, two geometries (FALCON; "six hotspots" is also the exact figure logged at Abqaiq 7/27, noted and NOT treated as corroboration). **FALCON tell #2 (D-indicator #2, "a second, larger salvo at Yanbu — or the Petroline") NOT FIRED, PENDING-CONFIRMATION** on the desk's standing anti-false-fire bar (declaration never fires; two claimant-independent confirmations as at Jazan 7/25; WALTER ADD#15 — no FIRMS claim without published coordinates; date-check first — the 2019 "stopped pumping" and April-2026 "restored to full capacity" traps are both live in search; "a hit is not a loss"). The entire 9/17–9/25 measurement period would therefore sit INSIDE the outage rather than across a recovery. ### §2.5b — What the curve prices for the PATH, not the decision (BOND, 2026-09-14 ~13:2x ET; `analysis/2026-09-14_what-the-curve-prices-for-the-PATH-not-the-decision.md`, KB-BND-283/284)
- ★ **THE CURVE ALREADY PRICES ~115–130bp OF FURTHER TIGHTENING — about 4.5–5 more 25bp hikes — TERMINAL ~4.75–4.95%, AND NO CUT INSIDE THREE YEARS.** Implied forward average overnight rate off par yields [H.15 **9/10** frontier; spot DFF 3.63 / IORB 3.65]: **yr1 4.28 (+65bp) · yr2 4.84 (+121bp) · yr3 4.77 · yr4–5 4.93.** **Year 3 sits 7bp BELOW year 2 ⇒ a shallow PLATEAU, not an easing cycle.** Stripping a ~15bp front term premium still leaves ~115bp ≈ 4.6 hikes.
- ⚖️ **THE ASYMMETRY, AND IT RUNS AGAINST OUR OWN BOOK.** Because the hawkish path is largely **PAID FOR**, a hawkish dot plot must exceed a **~4.75–4.95% terminal** to be a hawkish SURPRISE — a high bar. **A DOVISH plot (terminal <4.75, or any 2027 cut pencilled) is a LARGE repricing.**
- 🔑 **And this follows from BOND's own measurement rather than from a view: the move that fired the 004 add-gate WAS a terminal/policy-path repricing. What the policy path drove, the policy path unwinds — A DOVISH SEP WOULD UNWIND THE VERY MOVE THAT PUT `DFII10` THROUGH 2.50.** Same driver on both sides, which is precisely why the level firing is **not evidence for** the duration thesis.
- 🔴 **It is also the only plausible near-term route to `GATE-TERRY-007`: DGS10 4.95 needs a 45bp RALLY before its <4.50 count can even START.** ⇒ **Wednesday is SIMULTANEOUSLY the main risk to the 004 puts AND the only realistic near-term path to their registered exit.** BOND stated this as TERRY's lane, not as a proposal. $0 moved.
- 🔑 **BOND'S OWN CORRECTION TO ITS EARLIER DIAGNOSIS, SENT UNPROMPTED ~1h LATER — THE 9/10 MOVE PEAKED IN THE BELLY, NOT AT THE FRONT.** Full curve: **1M +10 · 3M +5 · 6M +6 · 1Y +11 · 2Y +13 · 3Y +14 · 5Y +14 · 7Y +13 · 10Y +12 · 20Y +11 · 30Y +9.** Peak at **3Y–5Y**, decaying BOTH ways, and **THE 3M MOVED LEAST OF THE ENTIRE CURVE (+5bp).** ⇒ A September-MEETING repricing peaks at 3M–1Y · a TERM-PREMIUM move peaks at the long end · **a TERMINAL-RATE repricing peaks in the BELLY.** **The near-flat 3M is direct evidence the September hike was ALREADY in the price and what repriced was the PATH BEYOND IT.**
- ⚠️ **This SHARPENS and does not retract the "front-led (ΔDGS2 +13 > ΔDGS30 +9)" wording — that comparison is true and stands.** ★★ **The fleet-wide lesson BOND drew against itself: A TWO-POINT SLOPE TEST REPORTS A MOVE'S SIGN AND CAN NEVER SEE ITS SHAPE.** It diagnosed a belly-peaked move from a 2Y-vs-30Y pair, **which cannot detect a belly by construction.** WALTER's independent 5-session curve vector peaks at **DGS5 +21** — same shape, different window, so two instruments agree on the belly over two windows. `KB-BND-284`.
- ✅ **FALSIFIER REGISTERED BEFORE THE PRINT, deliberately:** a **SEP median terminal ≥ ~5.00% with the curve unchanged** means the hawkish path was NOT fully priced and BOND's asymmetry read is **WRONG**. Checkable **2:00pm Wednesday**; carried as BOND SCRATCH next-session item 0b so it cannot quietly lapse.
- ⚠️ **LIMITS, stated by BOND so this is not over-read:** par-yield (CMT) forwards, **not** a bootstrapped zero curve; the ~15bp front term premium is **assumed and shown separately, not modelled**; and **this is the TREASURY CURVE'S implied path, NOT an OIS / fed-funds-futures strip.** They are different instruments and can disagree — **treat it as an independent cross-check, never as the priced probability, and never average it with a prediction-market number.** The actual futures strip is still owed (`KB-BND-282`, re-test 9/16). Every Treasury figure is on the **9/10** frontier and says so on its own row.

**9/17 14:3x ET — wording correction to hot amendment #3 (CATO `AGENTS/CATO/runs/2026-09-17_1413_prome-completed-work-review.md`, Medium #2):** the funding-leg result was first carried as an "adequately-powered null". BOND's pre-registration (`c563a9ff6` §5) fixed a MINIMUM-COUNT floor (n<10 = underpowered), not a power calculation, so n=19 clearing it establishes only that the test was not disqualified. The supported statement: **the pre-registered primary comparison did not detect support for H2 in this sample (n=19 paired / 33 unpaired; median −3.0bp vs 0.0; p=0.523); adequate power to exclude a meaningful effect has not been established.** A large p-value does not establish no effect. "H2 not confirmed" is supported; "genuine null, not an underpowered one" is the unsupported upgrade and originates in BOND's RESULT file (lines 7, 27, 77) — BOND told 14:3x, its files are its own. **BOND accepted in full and went further (`e3ce6e40f`, 14:37 ET): a post-hoc power simulation of the exact design gives MDE ≈14–15bp at 80% power (14% at 3bp · 18% at 5 · 30% at 8 · 46% at 10 · 68% at 12 · 88% at 15); the observed −3.0bp had ~14% power and the stock leg's own −11.5bp only ~60%. Every effect in the funding table is smaller than the MDE ⇒ the WHOLE table is underpowered against effects of the observed size; the n=19-vs-n=8/6 distinction was BOND's and was wrong. Six instances swept in its RESULT file + KB-BND-308. Hot amendment #3's "a meaningful effect is NOT excluded" stands and is now quantified here. BOND's forward rule: pre-registrations fix an MDE, never a count.** **Re-run at the REGISTERED α=0.01 (`30671c743`, 2026-09-17 15:2x ET, after CATO's follow-up caught the first simulation at α=0.05 and under-resolved): MDE ≈16bp (75% power at 15bp, 93% at 18bp; 300 trials × 1,500 resamples); the observed −3.0bp detectable about a fifth of the time; the STOCK leg's −11.5bp at ≈35–40% power — so the p=0.009 inversion also rests on a design that would miss its own effect more often than not. BOND's second forward rule: when correcting a statistical claim, re-read the pre-registration for the operative α — the correction pass is where a conventional default slips back in. Fifth external catch on BOND today; its instruments verify values, the errors lived in the words and thresholds around them.**

### §19.7 — War theaters, long form
**Will's 2026-09-18 question — are NATO/EU–Russia conflict chances rising — worked by HAWK, OSPREY and HANS.** ★ **The answer is CONTAMINATED, and contaminated is not reassuring.** The armed-INCIDENT risk is genuinely rising; the DELIBERATE-war risk is not visibly rising.
**ZERO of four 2026 Baltic engagements were confirmed clean Russian probes** — OSPREY, at the Estonian, Latvian and Lithuanian defence ministries. This is STRONGER than HAWK's morning reading of "two of four". ⛔ **Contaminated ≠ low: NATO firing more often at objects whose origin it cannot establish is a HIGHER accident risk than the raw count implies. It kills the inference about Russian INTENT, not the RISK.**
⚠️ **On the 2026-08-01 mandate conversion, quote the owner and not the summary.** OSPREY's own reading is that it **WEAKENS** the confound argument — the 05-19 and 06-08 engagements happened under the OLD mandate — and that **at most HALF** the series is explicable by a loosened trigger. ⛔ *"A large part of why"* overstates the owner **and in the opposite rhetorical direction**; do not write it.
⚠️ **A formulation is DEAD: *"SACEUR → CAOC Uedem → cockpit"*** — HAWK withdrew it as traceable only to an aggregator; the Ankara Summit Declaration contains no such text. The substance survives at the Estonian MoD and ERR; the NATO-document framing does not.
🔑 **Article 4 is FUNCTIONALLY SUPERSEDED.** 2025: invoked 9/10 → Eastern Sentry 9/12. 2026: that response already stands and expands with nobody invoking, so a requester gains nothing. ⇒ **its silence is weak evidence in BOTH directions and is NEVER reassurance.** Two clean facts survive with no Ukraine leg: **Leipzig/Halle** (a Russian state agency on NATO soil) and **Neptun Deep**.
**HANS, from the opposite direction: governments are acting as if conflict risk is rising and sovereign markets are not.** The Bund 10Y sits at a 15-year high (3.50 / 3.5187, observed 2026-09-18) while Germany amends its constitution to borrow for defence. ⚠️ **That is CONVERGENCE OF TWO UNRELATED INSTRUMENTS, never "two desks agree."** ⛔ **HANS'S CAVEAT, FLAGGED BY HANS AS ONE THAT MUST REACH THE OPERATOR IN SUBSTANCE AND NOT IN A FILE HE HAS TO OPEN: *"not priced" is NOT "not happening" — sovereign markets were not pricing February 2022 either.***
**A structural argument PROME initially missed and Will supplied:** the fleet is keyed by TRANSMISSION CHANNEL, so each desk sees one slice of one actor. In one week the same state seized four European companies' Russian operations, ran a sub-threshold armoured exercise on NATO's only land link to the Baltics, and sat on a mobilisation decision pending its own election — **HAWK saw airspace, OSPREY saw strikes, HANS saw spreads, and nobody saw the ACTOR.** That is the case for the YURI desk (WQ-267, seat unruled).
